<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM代理训练中纯行为克隆存在的训练与测试时上下文不匹配问题，并探索在有限监督预算下如何最优分配教师资源。

Method: 将在线策略数据构建形式化为预算分配问题，通过 rollout 策略、切换时间分布、延续视界、过滤规则及两种互补成本（过滤前教师推理成本、保留用于SFT的教师监督成本）来系统化设计空间。

Result: 在HotpotQA、ALFWorld和Terminal-Bench-Dev上，学习者诱导上下文中有限且未过滤的教师延续优于纯行为克隆。在HotpotQA和ALFWorld上，少量步骤的延续匹配或超越了成功过滤和关键上下文过滤的替代方案。

Conclusion: 少量教师步骤放置在学习者诱导上下文中，比更长或更严格筛选的教师完成更具成本效益，为LLM代理的监督分配提供了实用指导。

Abstract: For LLM agents, supervised fine-tuning is not only about teacher labels' quality, but also about which interaction contexts those labels condition on. Pure behavioral cloning uses full teacher demonstrations, creating a mismatch between teacher-induced contexts seen in training and student-induced contexts encountered at test time. Recent work addresses this mismatch by querying a teacher at contexts reached by the student, often with increasingly elaborate filtering of the teacher's continuations. We instead frame on-policy data construction as a budget-allocation problem: under matched supervision resources, should teacher output be spent on more start-to-finish demos, longer continuations, outcome filtering, or broader coverage of learner-induced contexts? We formalize this design space through the rollout policy, switch-time distribution, continuation horizon, filtering rules, and two complementary costs: teacher inference generated before filtering and teacher supervision retained for SFT. Across HotpotQA, ALFWorld, and Terminal-Bench-Dev, bounded unfiltered teacher continuations at learner-induced contexts improve over pure behavioral cloning at matched budgets. On HotpotQA and ALFWorld, where we run the full comparison, few-step continuations match or exceed success-filtered and critical-context-filtered alternatives. Our findings suggest that a few teacher steps, placed at learner-induced contexts, can be a more cost-efficient supervision allocation than longer or more heavily curated teacher completions.

</details>


### [370] [Beyond the Need for Speed: Energy-Aware Code Generation via Simulation-Guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04577)
*Saurabhsingh Rajput,Tushar Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用确定性架构仿真替代硬件测量，构建了包含350万次评估的Green Tea语料库，通过对比监督微调和闭环强化学习（GRPO）训练节能代码模型，并引入CARET指标评估部署就绪度。在143个保留问题上实现了12.63%的CARET提升，58.4%的输出优于人类专家基准。同时发现IPC指标在67.8%的问题上误导真实能效评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码模型仅关注功能正确性，忽略了软件能效优化。物理硬件测量因方差大无法提供可重现的大规模反馈，亟需一种确定性、可扩展的能效评估方法。

Method: 使用确定性架构仿真替代硬件测量构建Green Tea语料库（1474个C++问题，350万次评估）；通过能效对比监督微调+闭环强化学习（GRPO）训练；提出CARET指标（正确性调整的能耗总降幅）用于评估。

Result: 143个保留问题上实现12.63% CARET提升，是单独微调的近3倍；58.4%有效输出优于人类专家。发现IPC指标在67.8%的问题上对真实能效排名错误。

Conclusion: 直接能耗仿真对节能代码生成至关重要，IPC等吞吐量代理指标不可靠。开源数据集和基础设施可节省26.3万CPU小时，推动社区发展节能代码模型。

Abstract: Code models strictly prioritize functional correctness, leaving software energy efficiency as an unoptimized byproduct. Training models to generate energy-efficient code requires reproducible feedback at scale, which physical hardware measurement cannot reliably provide due to variance.
  In this paper, we replace hardware profiling with a deterministic architectural simulation harness to build Green Tea, a corpus of $3.5$ million evaluations across $1{,}474$ C++ problems. We train an energy-aware code model via supervised fine-tuning on energy-contrastive pairs, followed by closed-loop reinforcement learning (GRPO) using simulation-in-the-loop feedback. To rigorously evaluate deployment readiness, we introduce the Correctness-Adjusted Reduction in Energy Total (CARET), a metric that explicitly penalizes code that sacrifices functionality for efficiency.
  On $143$ held-out problems, our simulation-in-the-loop pipeline achieves $12.63\%$ CARET, nearly tripling the gain of fine-tuning alone, and successfully beats the energy efficiency of human-expert references on $58.4\%$ of its valid outputs. Furthermore, our analysis exposes the IPC trap: standard throughput proxies like Instructions-Per-Cycle (IPC) actively misrank true energy efficiency on $67.8\%$ of problems, proving the absolute necessity of direct energy simulation. By releasing our dataset and infrastructure, we bypass the $263{,}000$ CPU-hours required for reproduction, structurally empowering the community to deploy inherently energy-efficient code generation models.

</details>


### [371] [Score Distributions, Not Cells: Evaluating Single-Cell Perturbations Under Class Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04595)
*Youssef Marrakchi,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 单细胞扰动数据的分类问题中，传统逐细胞准确率因细胞群体重叠而失效（F1仅0.2-0.3）。本文提出Classifier Discrimination Score (CDS)：对扰动所有细胞的预测概率取平均作为群体特征进行排序，无需重新训练，线性时间复杂度，在Tahoe-100M和Virtual Cell Challenge上显著优于逐细胞准确率和原始伪批量方法，尤其在细胞稀缺时差距更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞扰动数据中，不同扰动产生的细胞群体高度重叠，导致逐细胞分类准确率无法反映模型质量（线性分类器、MLP、Transformer的macro-F1均仅0.2-0.3），亟需能评估群体层面区分能力的指标。

Method: 对分类器输出的每个细胞的概率向量，在扰动层面取平均得到群体特征（population profile），据此对候选扰动进行排序，得到Classifier Discrimination Score (CDS)。取排名第一的类别即为预测扰动。该方法无需重新训练，时间复杂度为O(细胞数)。

Result: 在Tahoe-100M和Virtual Cell Challenge上，CDS从原本表现很弱的模型中恢复出近乎完美的扰动识别能力，且比PDS（在原始基因表达空间取平均）更可靠，细胞越稀缺时优势越明显。

Conclusion: 逐细胞准确率和原始伪批量评分在比较扰动模型时应谨慎使用，因为它们可能错误排序真实标签，进而误导模型评估。

Abstract: Most classification problems assume the classes are roughly separable, so that an individual sample can usually be assigned to one class. Single-cell perturbation data violates this assumption: two perturbations can produce different populations of cells while overlapping so much that an individual cell could belong to either. Per-cell accuracy then measures this overlap rather than model quality. We see this on Tahoe-100M and the Virtual Cell Challenge, where a linear classifier, an MLP, and a Transformer all plateau near macro-F1 0.2-0.3 even though almost every pair of perturbations is statistically distinguishable.
  The fix is to score perturbations across the whole population rather than cell by cell. We average a classifier's per-cell probability vectors over all cells of a perturbation to form a population profile, then rank candidate perturbations by this profile; we call the resulting score the Classifier Discrimination Score (CDS). Taking the top-ranked class recovers the winning perturbation. It needs no retraining, costs linear time in the number of cells, and recovers near-perfect identification from the same weak models. CDS differs from the pseudobulk-based Perturbation Discrimination Score (PDS) used in recent benchmarks only in where the average is taken, raw gene expression for PDS versus a learned discriminative space for CDS, and identifies the true perturbation more reliably on both datasets, with the gap widening as cells grow scarce. Because a metric that misranks the ground truth will misrank the models scored against it, per-cell accuracy and raw-pseudobulk scores should be used with caution when comparing perturbation models.

</details>


### [372] [Minimum Block Width for Universal Approximation by Residual Neural Networks with Inner Width One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04597)
*Qi Zhou,Xuan Zhou,Xiao-Song Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了残差神经网络的通用逼近性质，建立了块宽度的上下界，证明了在不同逼近意义下所需的最小块宽度，并给出了无法逼近所有目标函数的块宽度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究残差神经网络在多大程度上具备通用逼近能力，特别是块宽度这一关键结构参数对逼近性能的影响，以指导网络架构设计。

Method: 针对LeakyReLU、ReLU等激活函数，从理论上推导了块宽度的上下界；通过构造和分析网络结构，证明了不同逼近意义下所需的最小块宽度；并推广到一般激活函数族，给出无法逼近的下界条件。

Result: 内宽为1时，精确最小块宽度为max{dx, dy}（L^p逼近）；残差分支内宽为1时，块宽度为min{dx+dy, max{2dx+1, dy}}可实现一致逼近；块宽度小于max{dx, dy}时无法逼近所有目标函数。

Conclusion: 残差神经网络的通用逼近能力与块宽度密切相关，存在精确的阈值，低于该阈值无法实现充分逼近。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the universal approximation property of residual neural networks, and obtain some new results. For input and output dimensions $d_x$ and $d_y$, and LeakyReLU, ReLU, ReLU-like activation functions, the upper and lower bounds of the block width are established. To achieve $L^p$ approximation $(1\leq p <+\infty)$ on any compact domain, we show that the exact minimum block width is $\max\{d_x,d_y\}$ when the inner width is 1. Furthermore, we show that residual neural networks with block width $\min\{d_x+d_y, \max\{2d_x+1,d_y\}\}$ can achieve uniform approximation on any compact domain under the constraint that each residual branch has inner width 1. Besides, for any activation function family, we prove that residual neural networks with block width less than $\max\{d_x, d_y\}$ cannot approximate all target functions, both in the $L^p$ sense and the uniform sense, regardless of inner width.

</details>


### [373] [Measuring What Matters: A Unified Evaluation Framework for GNN Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04600)
*Francesco Paolo Nerini,Mirko Zaffaroni,Paolo Baracco,Gabriele Ciravegna,Alan Perotti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个无需真实标签假设的统一量化基准框架，用于评估图神经网络的可解释性方法，通过将表格可解释性指标适配到图数据，识别出帕累托最优的G-XAI方法，并提供了实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有G-XAI方法众多，但缺乏统一的评估标准和可信度评估方法，导致实践者难以选择合适的可解释性工具，阻碍了实际应用。

Method: 提出统一量化基准框架，将表格数据中的可解释性指标形式化适配到图数据，分别评估拓扑结构和节点特征两个独立维度，进行大规模基准实验。

Result: 识别出在多指标对和任务上始终处于帕累托前沿的稳健非支配解，同时确认没有单一解释器在所有指标上具有绝对优势。

Conclusion: 将研究发现提炼为可操作的G-XAI可用性指南，帮助机器学习从业者评估和部署可信赖的GNN管道。

Abstract: Graph eXplainable AI (G-XAI) is increasingly important for making Graph Neural Networks interpretable and accountable. While a growing number of explainers are available, choosing the right method and assessing the trustworthiness of its outputs remains unclear. Consistent evaluation practices and actionable guidance are still missing, hindering practical adoption. In this paper, we introduce a unified, quantitative benchmarking framework for G-XAI that requires no ground-truth assumptions. We formalize tabular explainability metrics for graph data, evaluating topological structure and node features as independent components. Our large-scale benchmarking study identifies explainers that consistently lie on the Pareto front across metric pairs and tasks, establishing robustly non-dominated solutions - while confirming that no single explainer achieves universal superiority. We distill our findings into actionable G-XAI usability guidelines to support Machine Learning practitioners in evaluating and deploying trustworthy GNN-based pipelines.

</details>


### [374] [Reliability and Identifiability in Persona-Trained Monte Carlo: Variance Decomposition, Stability Bounds, and the Identifiability of Heterogeneous News Reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04627)
*Salavat Ishbulatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为基于人格训练的蒙特卡洛模拟器（PTMC）建立了完整的统计理论框架，包括方差分解、最优预算分配、误差预算、异质性新闻反应的识别理论，以及两个分离定理，为市场结果函数估计提供了"可靠"的严格定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多智能体模拟的市场结果估计器缺乏严格的统计保证，"可靠"一词缺乏精确的数学定义，需要建立完整的统计理论框架来量化各类误差源。

Method: 将估计量方差分解为人格抽样方差和运行内方差，使用ANOVA无偏估计；通过耦合界量化分布误估和策略误差的传播；利用Jensen gap和Hausdorff矩问题识别异质性新闻反应；通过分离定理刻画PTMC相对于同质模拟器和简化形式预测器的优势条件。

Result: 建立了三项误差预算（MC收敛、最坏情况误差、大K平均场极限）；给出了异质性新闻反应的√n一致估计量和边界校正检验；证明了异质性识别在响应族未知时的尖锐失效条件。

Conclusion: PTMC在异质性存在时具有不可约的Jensen偏差下限，且Lucas批判可形式化为外推干预的极小极大极限，为基于模拟的市场分析提供了严格的统计基础。

Abstract: Persona-Trained Monte Carlo (PTMC) estimates distributions of market-outcome functionals by repeatedly simulating limit-order-book interaction among $K$ neural policy bots whose behavioral personas are drawn from a learned heterogeneity distribution $\mathcal{P}$. This paper develops the statistical theory that makes the word "reliable" precise for such estimators.
  We decompose estimator variance into a persona-draw component $σ_P^2$ and a within-run component $σ_w^2$, give unbiased ANOVA estimators of both, and derive the variance-optimal allocation of a fixed compute budget between outer persona draws and inner replications. A coupling-based stability bound quantifies how misestimation of $\mathcal{P}$ and error in the trained policy propagate into the estimand, yielding a three-term total-error budget whose terms are separately estimable; a uniform-in-horizon version holds under a Doeblin condition on the market chain.
  The main contribution is an identification theory for heterogeneous news reaction: under a fixed response nonlinearity, the aggregate impact curve $A(z)=\mathbb{E}_Q[g(ηz)]$ detects heterogeneous news sensitivity through a strict Jensen gap and identifies the distribution $Q$ locally via odd moments and Hausdorff determinacy, with sharp failure when the response family is unknown. We provide $\sqrt{n}$-consistent estimators and a boundary-corrected test of homogeneous news reaction. Two separation theorems delimit when PTMC is provably preferable to homogeneous-population simulators and reduced-form forecasters, formalizing an irreducible Jensen bias floor and the Lucas critique as a minimax limit on intervention extrapolation. All proofs are given in full; guarantees are classified as unconditional (Monte Carlo convergence), conditional worst-case (the error budget), or open (the large-$K$ mean-field limit).

</details>


### [375] [Machine Learning for Depression Screening and Intervention: an Original Circadian Rhythm Score-based Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04648)
*Bin Wang,Shuo Lian,Yuanyuan Hou,Dexian Wang,Peilan He,Feng Hong,Yanwei Yu,Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于昼夜节律评分（CRS）的抑郁筛查框架，通过多领域行为数据的联合表征和非负约束压缩，实现了高可解释性的抑郁风险预测，并在CHARLS数据集中取得了ROC-AUC=0.825的性能，同时识别了可干预的行为阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抑郁筛查方法面临三大挑战：昼夜节律指标的碎片化、模型可解释性差、以及缺乏干预导向的分析。传统方法孤立分析睡眠、活动和社交行为，未能捕捉其联合昼夜节律结构。

Method: 提出昼夜节律评分（CRS），通过非负约束压缩多领域日常行为为统一表征；基于梯度提升树和SHAP分析构建可解释筛查框架；结合交互建模和反事实回归估计异质性和剂量依赖性行为效应。

Result: 在CHARLS数据集（n=15,233）上ROC-AUC达0.825；单变量CRS几乎保留了原始多指标的全部预测能力；识别出约300 MET-min/周的最低有效运动剂量和约65分钟的最佳恢复性午睡时长。

Conclusion: 该框架桥接了监督表示学习与可解释建模，为抑郁筛查和干预感知的医疗数据挖掘提供了可扩展的方法论。

Abstract: Depression screening from large-scale behavioral data is challenged by fragmented circadian indicators, limited interpretability, and the lack of intervention-oriented analysis. Existing approaches typically analyze sleep, activity, and social behaviors in isolation, failing to capture their joint circadian structure. To address this limitation, we first propose the Circadian Rhythm Score (CRS), a composite index that compresses multi-domain daily behaviors into a unified representation of circadian rhythm. CRS is constructed to maximize discriminative power for depression screening while preserving behavioral semantics through non-negativity constraints. Empirical results demonstrate near-lossless compression, where a single CRS retains almost the full predictive capability compared with multiple raw behavioral indicators. Building upon CRS, we develop an interpretable depression screening framework based on gradient-boosted trees and SHAP analysis, revealing nonlinear and saturation-like associations between circadian rhythm and depression risk. Beyond risk prediction, we further integrate interaction modeling and counterfactual regression to estimate heterogeneous and dose-dependent behavioral effects, enabling intervention-oriented reasoning under different circadian contexts. Experiments on the China Health and Retirement Longitudinal Study (CHARLS, n=15,233), demonstrate robust screening performance (ROC-AUC=0.825) and identify actionable behavioral thresholds, including a minimum effective exercise dose of approximately 300 MET-min/week and an optimal restorative nap duration of approximately 65 minutes for sleep-deprived individuals. By bridging supervised representation learning and interpretable modeling, this work provides a scalable framework for depression screening and intervention-aware healthcare data mining.

</details>


### [376] [A Physics-Regulated Neural Framework for Learning 3D Grain Growth Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04680)
*Zhihui Tian,Kang Yang,Michael Tonks,Amanda R. Krause,Joel B. Harley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出3D-PRIMME模型，仅用两个时间步训练即可准确预测三维晶粒生长，保持线性粗化规律和拓扑统计量，且能外推到超大系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动代理模型在保持晶粒生长的物理不变量（如统计标度律）和计算可扩展性方面存在挑战，尤其在三维场景下。

Method: 3D-PRIMME（物理约束可解释机器学习）模型，在100³网格/512晶粒上训练，仅需两个连续时间步，学习尺度无关的局部演化规则。

Result: 模型可外推至1024³网格/550000晶粒（无需重训），准确再现线性粗化动力学，并保持晶粒拓扑分布长时间稳定。

Conclusion: 3D-PRIMME学习到了尺度无关且时间稳定的局部演化规则，实现了高效鲁棒的大规模三维微观结构演化代理预测。

Abstract: Grain growth is governed by the reduction in grain boundary energy and exhibits well-established statistical scaling laws. Developing data-driven surrogates that preserve these physical invariants while remaining computationally scalable remains challenging, especially in 3D. We present 3D-PRIMME (Physics-Regulated Interpretable Machine Learning for Microstructure Evolution) for learning three-dimensional grain growth dynamics. The model is trained using only two consecutive time steps yet accurately reproduces the linear coarsening law and preserves topological statistics over extended time scales. Despite being trained on a $100^3$ grid points with 512 grains, the learned evolution operator is applied to domains up to $1024^3$ grid points with 550000 grains without retraining, maintaining consistent kinetics and grain topology across orders-of-magnitude increases in system size. These results demonstrate that 3D-PRIMME learns a scale-independent and temporally stable local evolution rule, enabling efficient and robust large-scale surrogate prediction of 3D microstructure evolution.

</details>


### [377] [F-ACVAE: A Federated Adaptive Conditional Variational Auto-Encoder for Privacy-Preserving Intrusion Detection in IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04698)
*Mohammad Ansarimehr,Somayeh Changiz,Ehsan Baghishani,Ali Mousavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出F-ACVAE框架，通过联邦自适应条件变分自编码器实现分布式IoT设备协同训练，在N-BaIoT数据集上达到99%的准确率和宏F1分数，同时减少约62%通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集中式入侵检测系统在高维流量数据、极端类别不平衡及异构边缘设备上的非IID数据分布下性能严重下降，且存在隐私泄露风险。

Method: 提出F-ACVAE框架，包含选择性参数聚合（本地编码器保持私有，全局组件同步）和约束动量高斯聚合（CMGA）策略（更新钳制+动量平滑）以缓解客户端漂移。

Result: 在N-BaIoT数据集上平均准确率和宏F1分数达到99%，优于现有基线；选择性聚合机制减少约62%通信开销。

Conclusion: F-ACVAE在保障隐私和通信效率的同时，实现了高检测性能，适合资源受限的IoT环境。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Internet of things (IoT) devices has significantly expanded the cyber-attack surface, necessitating robust and privacy-preserving intrusion detection systems (IDS). However, centralized learning approaches often suffer from severe performance degradation due to high-dimensional traffic data, extreme class imbalance, and highly non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data across heterogeneous edge devices. To address these challenges, this paper proposes F-ACVAE, a federated adaptive conditional variational autoencoder framework that enables collaborative model training across distributed IoT devices without sharing raw data. F-ACVAE incorporates selective parameter aggregation, where local encoders remain private while globally shared components are synchronized to preserve discriminative latent structures. To further enhance stability under extreme non-IID settings and feature distribution shifts, we introduce a novel constrained momentum Gaussian aggregation (CMGA) strategy that combines update clamping with momentum-based smoothing to mitigate client drift. Extensive experiments on the N-BaIoT dataset demonstrate that F-ACVAE achieves an average accuracy and macro F1-score of 99\%, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. Moreover, the selective aggregation mechanism reduces communication overhead by approximately 62\%, making the framework particularly suitable for resource-constrained IoT environments. These results highlight the effectiveness of F-ACVAE in achieving high detection performance while ensuring privacy preservation and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [378] [Hierarchical Scaffolding Enables Human-Like Cognitive Selectivity under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04709)
*Juhyoung Park,Jaehyuk Bae,Hyeonbo Yang,Se-Bum Paik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCALA是一种受认知心理学启发的分层学习框架，通过从粗粒度概念结构到细粒度识别的引导式学习，在数据稀缺条件下实现了类似人类的样本效率和鲁棒类别泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习系统需要大量数据，而人类在数据极其有限的情况下仍能高效学习，这种效率差距激励作者探索一种更接近人类认知机制的学习方法。

Method: 提出SCALA分层学习框架，从粗粒度概念结构引导到细粒度识别，通过优先处理任务相关特征、抑制背景干扰来实现类似人类的认知选择性。

Result: SCALA在数据稀缺条件下显著提升了分类准确率，加速了聚类形成，减少了类内分散度，增强了语义可分离性，并在未见类别上展现了良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SCALA是实现数据受限环境下类人样本效率和鲁棒类别泛化的有效框架，为小样本学习提供了新的认知启发式解决方案。

Abstract: Modern machine learning systems demand extensive datasets for visual recognition. Conversely, humans learn with high efficiency despite severe data limitations, often by acquiring broad categorical structures before refining finer distinctions. Inspired by this contrast, we introduce SCALA (Scaffolded Cognitive Architecture for Learning under limited dAta), a hierarchical learning framework grounded in cognitive psychology that guides models from coarse conceptual structures to fine-grained recognition. Our model exhibits human-like cognitive selectivity by effectively prioritizing task-relevant features while suppressing background distractors, a mechanism that induces a fundamental shift in representation learning. This shift is characterized by accelerated cluster formation, reduced intra-class dispersion, and enhanced semantic separability. Empirically, SCALA achieves significant accuracy improvements under severe data scarcity. Furthermore, this hierarchical scaffolding promotes robust generalization to unseen classes and accelerates the acquisition of novel categories. Collectively, our results establish SCALA as a powerful framework for achieving human-level sample efficiency and resilient category generalization in data-constrained environments.

</details>


### [379] [RSPO: Reward-Swap Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04713)
*Qiang Liu,Taian Guo,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reward-Swap Policy Optimization (RSPO)方法，通过奖励交换机制结合密集过程奖励与稀疏结果奖励，解决多轮交互任务中奖励稀疏和代理奖励偏移问题，在WebShop和ALFWorld基准上显著提升多种强化学习算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮长序列任务中，稀疏结果奖励导致训练收敛慢、采样轨迹有限，而密集过程奖励虽能加速收敛但可能与真实结果奖励存在偏差，影响模型最终性能。作者希望结合二者优势，既利用过程奖励的丰富信息加速训练，又保证优化目标与真实结果奖励的一致性。

Method: 提出RSPO方法，核心是奖励交换机制（reward-swap mechanism）：在训练过程中动态切换使用过程奖励和结果奖励，确保轨迹采样多样性（通过过程奖励引导探索），同时优化目标与真实结果奖励保持一致。该方法可灵活集成到GRPO、PPO、GiGPO等强化学习算法中。

Result: 在WebShop和ALFWorld两个具身智能体基准任务上，将RSPO应用于GRPO、PPO和GiGPO算法，均取得一致且显著的性能提升，验证了方法的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: RSPO通过奖励交换机制有效融合了过程奖励的丰富信号与结果奖励的准确性，既加速了训练收敛，又提升了模型最终性能天花板，是一种通用且有效的多轮交互任务训练方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning holds significant potential for training large language models (LLMs) to handle multi-turn interactive tasks. However, in long-horizon, multi-turn tasks characterized by sparse outcome rewards, directly training with outcome rewards often results in slow convergence due to the sparsity of signals and the lack of fine-grained feedback. Furthermore, the model may fail to learn successful trajectories that are not sampled during training, thereby limiting its performance. Conversely, while employing customized dense process rewards provides richer signals and accelerates convergence, these surrogate rewards may exhibit potential misalignment with the ground-truth outcome rewards. This inconsistency can bias the training direction and ultimately degrade the model's final performance. In this work, we propose Reward-Swap Policy Optimization (RSPO), a method designed to leverage the rich information from dense process rewards to facilitate training with outcome rewards. By utilizing a reward-swap mechanism, RSPO ensures the diversity of sampled trajectories while guaranteeing consistency between the optimization objective and the true outcome rewards, thereby elevating the performance ceiling of the model. We conduct extensive experiments on two challenging agent benchmarks, WebShop and ALFWorld. By applying our method to various reinforcement learning algorithms, including GRPO, PPO, and GiGPO, we demonstrate that RSPO achieves consistent performance improvements across different baselines and benchmarks.

</details>


### [380] [Trust Region Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04751)
*Zhengpeng Xie,Li Lyna Zhang,Zeke Xie,Mao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Trust Region Policy Distillation (TOP-D)，通过动态构建近端教师模型，将不稳定的策略蒸馏（OPD）转化为稳定训练范式，在数学推理任务上显著提升训练稳定性、样本效率和最终性能，且零额外计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统On-Policy Distillation (OPD)存在不稳定、高方差的问题，难以直接应用于复杂任务。作者希望通过动态构建近端教师模型来稳定训练过程。

Method: 提出Trust Region Policy Distillation (TOP-D)，通过动态构建近端教师模型来替代传统OPD中的固定教师模型，从而控制梯度方差。

Result: 在数学推理任务上，TOP-D显著提升了训练稳定性、样本效率和最终性能，且未引入额外计算开销。

Conclusion: TOP-D通过理论保证（全局收敛分析和单调改进界）和实证验证，证明其作为OPD的可靠替代方案具有重要价值。

Abstract: Big goals are hard to achieve all at once; breaking them into small steps is wiser. We present Trust Region Policy Distillation (TOP-D), which transforms the notoriously unstable, high-variance On-Policy Distillation (OPD) into a stable training paradigm by dynamically constructing a proximal teacher. Theoretically, we establish a rigorous framework demonstrating that TOP-D inherently controls gradient variance. By providing a formal global convergence analysis alongside a monotonic improvement bound, we mathematically formalize the reliability and stability of the overall training dynamics. Empirically, TOP-D dramatically enhances training stability, sample efficiency, and final performance on mathematical reasoning tasks. More importantly, TOP-D introduces zero additional computational overhead, positioning itself as a promising alternative to the well-established OPD paradigm.

</details>


### [381] [MARLIN: De Novo Molecular Structure Elucidation from Tandem Mass Spectra without a Ground-Truth Formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04774)
*Xujun Che,Xiuxia Du,Depeng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MARLIN是一种无需分子式即可从质谱中直接进行从头结构解析的方法，通过自监督编码器和块扩散语言模型生成候选结构，在NPLIB1基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非靶向串联质谱检测到大量小分子，但大多数因不在谱库中而无法鉴定，而这些未表征的代谢物和天然产物恰是药物发现、生物标志物研究和暴露组学中最重要的化合物。现有从头解析方法几乎都假设已知分子式，这在实际中不可行。

Method: MARLIN包含自监督编码器从原始峰预测分子指纹，块扩散语言模型仅基于指纹和前驱体质量生成候选结构；采用质量壳约束确保候选结构与测量质量一致；使用对称噪声目标吸收编码器误差，以及候选多样性机制避免结果坍缩。

Result: 在NPLIB1基准上，MARLIN在不使用真实分子式的情况下，在精确匹配准确率、结构距离和指纹相似度方面均表现最佳，并且作为副产品恢复正确分子式的频率与专用预测器相当。

Conclusion: MARLIN能够在分子式不可用的现实发现场景中实现可靠的从头结构解析。

Abstract: Untargeted tandem mass spectrometry (MS/MS) detects thousands of small molecules per biological sample, yet most go unidentified because they are absent from spectral libraries. These uncharacterized metabolites and natural products are precisely the compounds that matter for drug discovery, biomarker research, and exposomics. Computational de novo structure elucidation could close this gap, but almost all state-of-the-art methods assume the ground-truth molecular formula is known, an oracle that does not exist for genuinely novel compounds and is itself predicted with substantial error. We present MARLIN, a de novo method that elucidates structures directly from a spectrum with no molecular formula at any stage. A self-supervised encoder predicts a molecular fingerprint from the raw peaks, and a block-diffusion language model generates candidate structures conditioned only on the fingerprint and the instrument-measured precursor mass. A provably safe mass-shell constraint keeps every candidate consistent with the measured mass without fixing the atom inventory, and candidates are accepted by exact parts-per-million mass agreement. A symmetric noise objective absorbs encoder error, and a candidate-diversity mechanism keeps the candidates from collapsing to a single structure. On the NPLIB1 benchmark, MARLIN is the strongest method evaluated without a ground-truth formula across exact-match accuracy, structural distance, and fingerprint similarity, and it recovers the correct molecular formula as a byproduct about as often as a dedicated predictor without ever using one. MARLIN enables reliable de novo structure elucidation in the realistic discovery regime where the molecular formula is unavailable.

</details>


### [382] [Towards Personalized Differentially Private Learning for Decentralized Local Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04777)
*Longzhu He,Peng Tang,Chaozhuo Li,Jinhu Fu,Litian Zhang,Li Sun,Philip S. Yu,Sen Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PPGNN框架，在去中心化图数据学习中实现个性化差分隐私保护，通过用户自定义隐私预算和加权校准策略平衡隐私与效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LDP的图学习方法采用统一的隐私预算，忽略了真实系统中用户异质化的个性化隐私需求，导致噪声注入不灵活、图数据失真严重、下游分析效用下降。

Method: 提出PPGNN框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 个性化扰动机制(PPM)，允许用户设定个性化隐私预算进行本地扰动；2) 加权校准策略FlexProp，处理异构隐私水平下的噪声失真问题。

Result: 在六个真实图数据集上的实验表明，PPGNN能够有效平衡个性化隐私保护与数据效用。

Conclusion: PPGNN成功解决了去中心化图学习场景中个性化隐私保护与数据可用性的权衡问题，为异构隐私偏好下的图数据采集与分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Graph-structured data is increasingly generated and stored in decentralized environments, such as social platforms, mobile applications, and edge networks, where users maintain control over their local graph data. However, collecting and analyzing such decentralized graph data for downstream learning tasks raises significant privacy concerns, as nodes and their attributes often contain sensitive personal information. Local Differential Privacy (LDP) has emerged as a promising solution for privacy-preserving data collection without relying on trusted servers. Nevertheless, existing LDP-based graph learning methods typically assume uniform privacy requirements across users, ignoring the heterogeneous and personalized privacy preferences commonly observed in real-world systems. This uniform treatment leads to inflexible noise injection at the data collection stage, resulting in substantial distortion of graph data and degraded utility in subsequent analysis. To address this limitation, we propose PPGNN, a personalized differentially private framework for decentralized graph data. PPGNN enables user-specific privacy budgets during local perturbation while preserving analytical utility. To handle heterogeneous privacy levels and noise distortion, we design a two-stage solution consisting of a Personalized Perturbation Mechanism (PPM) and a weighted calibration strategy, FlexProp. Extensive experiments on six real-world graph datasets demonstrate that PPGNN effectively balances personalized privacy protection and data utility in decentralized graph learning scenarios.

</details>


### [383] [Predicting Drafted Deck Strength for "Magic: the Gathering"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04782)
*Tomas Rigaux,Hisashi Kashima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种编码器模型，用于《万智牌》轮抽（Draft）格式中的结果预测，通过生成牌面嵌入来处理动态变化的规则集，在大型真实数据集上优于线性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多真实游戏（如《万智牌》）的规则由不断变化的牌面动态定义，缺乏固定的紧凑规则集，使得通用策略学习不切实际。轮抽格式将选牌过程与游戏过程隔离，为研究组合协同驱动的决策提供了一个可控而具有挑战性的场景。

Method: 提出一个基于编码器的模型，为每张牌生成牌组上下文相关的嵌入，以编码轮抽决策序列。该模型利用大型真实世界数据进行训练和评估。

Result: 在大型真实数据上，该模型始终优于线性基线，建立了《万智牌》轮抽结果预测的第一个学习型基准。

Conclusion: 编码器模型能够有效捕捉动态游戏环境中的组合协同关系，为类似复杂决策问题提供了可行的学习框架。

Abstract: Many real-world games do not admit a fixed, compact rule set: instead, their dynamics are defined by interactions among a large and often evolving collection of game pieces, making general-purpose policy learning impractical. Magic: the Gathering (MTG) exemplifies this setting, where the cards themselves define and alter gameplay rules, strategic constraints, and long-term outcomes, while the pool of available cards is ever-changing. We study Draft, a constrained deck-building format of MTG in which eight players make 39-45 sequential selections from semi-random packs to construct a 40-card deck under partial information. By isolating the card selection process from gameplay, Draft provides a tractable yet non-trivial setting for studying decision-making driven by combinatorial card synergies. We propose an encoder-based model that produces set-contextualized card embeddings to encode the draft decision sequence, with a consistent improvement over linear baselines on large-scale real-world data, establishing a first learned benchmark for outcome prediction in MTG Draft. Our code is available at github.com/akulen/MtGDraftEncoder.

</details>


### [384] [Compressed Computation under $L^4$ Loss is likely Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04800)
*Francisco Ferreira da Silva,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经网络在超定位（superposition）下的计算能力，提出压缩计算框架：用50个神经元的单隐层ReLU网络计算100个稀疏输入特征的ReLU。通过L4损失训练，网络学会了用稀疏二进制码字编码每个特征，并用编码器的伪逆解码。最终用三个标量即可描述该网络的大部分性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络已知能以方向编码概念（表征超定位），但能否在神经元数量少于待计算函数数量的情况下进行计算（计算超定位）尚不明确。现有研究缺乏通过训练而非手工设计产生的计算超定位玩具模型。

Method: 采用压缩计算框架：单隐层ReLU网络（50个神经元）需计算100个稀疏输入特征的ReLU。使用L4损失（误差四次方的均值）而非传统L2损失进行训练。训练后对网络进行逆向工程分析，发现网络为每个特征分配稀疏二进制码字，并用编码器伪逆进行解码。

Result: L4损失训练能促使网络产生超定位计算解；网络性能可被仅含三个标量的描述高度复现；通过手工设计码字构建的等效网络验证了该发现。

Conclusion: 神经网络能在超定位下进行有效计算，且这种计算可通过稀疏编码和线性解码机制解释。L4损失是诱导超定位计算的关键因素。

Abstract: Neural networks are thought to represent concepts as directions in their activation space, and superposition lets them encode more concepts than they have dimensions. It is natural to ask whether they can also compute more functions than they have neurons, i.e., perform computation in superposition. In this regime many functions of sparse inputs are evaluated by a layer with fewer neurons than there are functions to compute. Representation in superposition is by now fairly well understood, but computation in superposition is not, and there are few toy models of it arising through training rather than being hand designed. As a toy model of computation in superposition we study the compressed-computation setup: a single-hidden-layer ReLU network with 50 neurons that must compute the ReLU of each of 100 sparse input features. We show that training it under an $L^4$ loss (the mean fourth power of the error), rather than the usual $L^2$, elicits a solution that appears to compute all features in superposition. We then reverse-engineer this solution. We find that the network assigns each feature a sparse binary codeword over neurons and decodes it with a pseudoinverse of the encoder. Given these codewords, a description with only three scalars recovers most of the network's performance, and we validate it by building equivalent networks from hand-designed codes.

</details>


### [385] [Layer-Parallel Inference Reduces Encrypted Nonlinear Depth in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04819)
*Ligong Han,Kai Xu,Hao Wang,Ruijiang Gao,Akash Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化牛顿层并行性（SNLP），将Transformer的顺序非线性层压缩为少量求解器迭代加线性修正，在FHE推理中实现2.65倍符号引导减少，仅损失1.2%困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全同态加密（FHE）下的Transformer推理受限于非线性块顺序组合带来的性能瓶颈，需要降低层间顺序非线性深度。

Method: 提出SNLP方法，用少量求解器迭代加线性结构化修正替代L层顺序非线性；基于Chebyshev多项式近似构建仿真框架进行误差分析。

Result: 在0.5B模型上，符号引导从53降至20（2.65倍），困惑度仅上升1.2%；误差放大因子1.36倍（顺序为1.42倍）；所有测试模型上SNLP的误差放大均低于顺序推理。

Conclusion: SNLP能有效降低FHE推理开销，且与块级FHE友好算子设计互补；softmax近似是主要误差来源，CKKS算术噪声影响可忽略。

Abstract: Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) enables computation on encrypted data, but practical encrypted Transformer inference is bottlenecked by the sequential composition of many nonlinear blocks. We study whether Structured Newton Layer Parallelism (SNLP) can make this inter-layer composition more FHE-friendly: each Transformer block still requires polynomial approximations for operations such as softmax and RMSNorm, but SNLP reduces the layerwise sequential nonlinear depth from L stages to a small number of solver iterations plus linear structured corrections. Using a simulation framework based on Chebyshev polynomial approximations, we measure error accumulation under sequential versus SNLP inference across 8 models and 4 architecture families. On a 0.5B IDN-trained model, SNLP reduces symbolic bootstraps from 53 to 20 (2.65x) with only +1.2% perplexity degradation, while lowering error amplification (1.36x vs. 1.42x). Across all tested models, SNLP has lower amplification than sequential inference. Ablations show that softmax approximation dominates the error budget and CKKS arithmetic noise is negligible in our setting, suggesting that SNLP is complementary to block-level FHE-friendly operator design rather than a replacement for it.

</details>


### [386] [KinEMbed: Decoding Kinematics from Electromyography via Cross-Modal Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04820)
*Sofia Gilardini,Chenfei Ma,Kianoush Nazarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KinEMbed，一种跨模态对比学习框架，用于从表面肌电信号回归手部运动学（关节角度）。该方法在NinaPro DB8数据集上优于PCA、PLS、自编码器和CEBRA等基线方法，尤其对拇指关节的预测提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EMG表示学习多聚焦于离散手势分类，缺乏对连续运动学回归的研究。

Method: 提出KinEMbed框架，采用跨模态对比学习联合训练双编码器（EMG特征编码器和运动学目标编码器），使EMG嵌入继承运动学空间的几何结构，推理时无需运动学信号。

Result: 在NinaPro DB8数据集（N=11，含健全者和肢体差异者）上，KinEMbed在留出会话评估中优于PCA、PLS、自编码器和CEBRA基线，对拇指关节自由度预测提升最大。

Conclusion: 该工作是迈向从结构化可穿戴生物信号对比学习手部运动学回归的第一步。

Abstract: Decoding hand kinematics from surface electromyography (EMG) is a core challenge in wearable biosignal processing with clinical relevance for prosthetic control and motor rehabilitation. Most representation learning approaches for EMG focus on discrete gesture classification, and few focus on continuous regression. We present KinEMbed, a cross-modal contrastive learning framework for hand kinematics regression that jointly trains dual encoders -- one for windowed EMG features and one for kinematic (joint angle) targets. The resulting embeddings inherit the geometric structure of the kinematic space without requiring kinematic signals at inference time. Evaluating on the NinaPro DB8 dataset that includes both able-bodied users and subjects with limb difference (N=11), KinEMbed outperforms PCA, PLS, autoencoder and contrastive (CEBRA) baselines on held-out sessions, with largest gains on the most challenging thumb degrees of articulation. We position this work as a first step toward contrastive representation learning for regression of hand kinematics from structured wearable biosignals.

</details>


### [387] [Probably Correct Optimal Stable Matching under Two-Sided Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04824)
*Andreas Athanasopoulos,Anne-Marie George,Christos Dimitrakakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了两侧偏好未知的双边市场中稳定匹配的序列学习问题，提出了基于"普适稳定匹配"概念的消除算法，在纯探索和遗憾最小化两种场景下实现了高效识别最优稳定匹配的目标，并给出了精细的样本复杂度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要假设至少一侧的偏好已知，或未充分利用部分偏好信息。本文旨在处理两侧偏好均不确定的情况，并利用部分偏好结构信息，从而更高效地识别最优稳定匹配。

Method: 引入"普适稳定匹配"概念，基于部分偏好信息识别最优稳定匹配；设计带有停止准则的消除算法，该准则充分利用学习到的部分偏好结构；在纯探索和遗憾最小化两个场景下给出相应算法与分析。

Result: 在纯探索场景下，算法能高效以高概率识别最优稳定匹配；在遗憾最小化场景下，建立了不依赖于最小奖励间隙Δ_min的遗憾上界。

Conclusion: 通过利用部分偏好信息和普适稳定匹配的概念，可以在两侧未知的双边市场中更高效地学习最优稳定匹配，且遗憾分析摆脱了对最小间隙的依赖，具有更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We study a sequential learning problem for stable matchings in two-sided markets where preferences on both sides are initially unknown. We focus on a centralized setting where an algorithm matches agents at each time step and receives noisy rewards that reflect the preferences of the matched agents, following a semi-bandit feedback structure. We adopt a pure exploration perspective, aiming to efficiently identify the optimal stable matching with high probability. Our work extends prior results by handling \emph{two-sided uncertainty} and by exploiting \emph{partial preference} information. A central ingredient is the notion of \textbf{pervasive stable matching}, which enables the identification of optimal stable matchings under partial preferences. We propose elimination-based algorithms whose stopping criteria exploit the structure of the learned partial preferences, and provide a refined sample-complexity analysis. Beyond pure exploration, we extend our approach to regret minimization and establish regret bounds with respect to the \emph{optimal} stable matching that avoid dependence on the minimum reward gap $Δ_{\min}$.

</details>


### [388] [Representing and Detecting Label Ambiguity in IMU-Based Exercise Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04842)
*Andreas Spilz,Heiko Oppel,Michael Munz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于标签分布的学习方法，用于从IMU数据自动评估家庭物理治疗动作，能够识别边界模糊的重复动作并生成其模糊度信息，在四个数据集上达到或超越传统分类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭物理治疗中无人监督导致动作执行不正确，现有系统将每个重复动作硬分类到单一类别，但大量边界样本即使专家也存在分歧，传统独热编码分类器丢失了这种模糊性信息。

Method: 提出一种无需大量标注者即可自动生成每个重复动作标签分布的方法，使用KL散度目标训练网络以再现完整标签分布（模糊方法），并与独热交叉熵基线在四个IMU运动数据集上进行比较。

Result: 模糊方法在四个数据集上均达到或超过基线分类性能，并能更可靠地检测模糊性及相关类别。

Conclusion: 在训练目标中引入标签分布表示可以在不牺牲分类性能的前提下提供额外的模糊度信息。

Abstract: Home-based physiotherapy is performed without supervision, which leads to incorrect execution and motivates systems that assess movement automatically from inertial measurement units (IMUs). Such systems assign each repetition to a category, yet a relevant share of repetitions falls near a class boundary, where even trained raters disagree. Classifiers trained with one-hot labels collapse these borderline repetitions onto a single class and discard this ambiguity. We address this with a method that automatically generates a label distribution per repetition without a large rater pool. We train a network to reproduce the full distribution with a Kullback-Leibler objective, the ambiguity approach, and compare it against a one-hot cross-entropy baseline on four IMU exercise datasets. From the network output we further determine whether a repetition is ambiguous and which classes are relevant to it. The ambiguity approach matched or exceeded the baseline classification on all four datasets, and detected ambiguity and the relevant classes more reliably. Representing the label distribution in the training target therefore adds information about ambiguity at no cost to classification.

</details>


### [389] [Pretraining Curricula Enable Selective Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04846)
*Sebastian A. Bruijns,Jirko Rubruck,Mia H. Whitefield,Kai J. Sandbrink,Fazl Barez,Christopher Summerfield*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，在预训练阶段采用不平衡课程（一个任务先学，另一个后学）相比平衡课程，能促进上下文学习、提高拒绝微调的选择性，并鼓励任务在分离的神经回路中解耦表达，从而提升安全微调的精确性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索预训练阶段中显式课程学习（平衡 vs. 不平衡）如何影响模型的学习、泛化能力以及微调的选择性，尤其关注AI安全中通过微调选择性抑制不良行为的问题。

Method: 比较平衡课程（均匀采样）与不平衡课程（一个任务先学，另一个后学）在两种冲突的复制任务上的效果，通过消融实验和激活补丁分析神经回路分离情况，并扩展到合成语言学习任务（规则一致与规则违反数据）。

Result: 不平衡课程促进了上下文学习，提高了拒绝微调的选择性；消融和激活补丁表明不平衡预训练鼓励任务在可分离的神经电路中解耦，而平衡训练则通过共同通路处理两个任务；在语言学习任务中，不平衡课程同样导致更局部化、更少纠缠的规则表示。

Conclusion: 不平衡预训练课程可能是一种促进解耦表示的重要工具，对安全微调的精确性和可靠性有直接积极影响。

Abstract: Transformers follow implicit curricula whereby some tasks are learned before others. However, how explicit pretraining curricula influence learning, generalization, and the selectivity of fine-tuning is unclear. This is important for AI safety, where fine-tuning is used to selectively suppress misaligned behaviors. Here, we compare curricula that pretrain tasks in a balanced (sampled uniformly) or an imbalanced (one task early, the other late) fashion. We show that imbalanced learning of two conflicting copy tasks promotes in-context learning and improves the selectivity of refusal fine-tuning. Ablations and activation patching show that this occurs because imbalanced pretraining encourages tasks to be disentangled in separable neural circuits, whereas balanced training routes both tasks through a common pathway. We extend these findings to a synthetic language learning task involving rule-consistent and rule-violating data, where imbalanced curricula similarly lead to more localized, less entangled rule representations, resulting in more robust rule-following behavior. Together, these results suggest that imbalanced pretraining curricula may be an important tool for promoting disentangled representations, with direct consequences for the precision and reliability of safety fine-tuning.

</details>


### [390] [Framework for Grouping Local Process Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04856)
*Viki Peeva,Wil M. P. van der Aalst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对局部过程模型（LPM）发现中模型爆炸和重复问题的分组框架，通过基于模型相似性或上下文属性选取代表性模型来优化样本，实验证明该方法比单纯使用高分模型能提供更少重复、更优覆盖的过程理解样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LPM发现算法会产生大量结构或行为相似的模型，分析师无法人工筛选数千个模型；而当前默认使用top-scoring模型作为最优样本的做法在高重复性下效果很差，无法有效支持过程理解。

Method: 提出一个LPM分组框架：通过过程模型相似度度量或基于事件日志中数据属性构建的上下文来度量LPM之间的相似性，将LPM分组后从每组中选取一个代表性模型形成最优样本。

Result: 在多个事件日志上实验，比较了top-scoring模型样本与分组代表性样本的重复率和覆盖率，证明了分组方法能显著降低重复性并提高对过程的理解覆盖。

Conclusion: 以理解过程为目标时，单纯依赖top-scoring LPMs不是好选择；提出的分组框架能有效缓解模型重复问题，使得样本更具代表性和可解释性。

Abstract: Local Process Models (LPMs) are an underexplored concept in process mining. LPMs describe patterns in event data considering sequence, choice, concurrency, and loop. In recent years, process mining has proved successful in the analysis and improvement of operational processes. More often than not, surprising findings are found when one does not consider the full process, making LPMs and their discovery highly valuable. However, similar to other pattern mining approaches, LPM discovery algorithms face the problems of model explosion and model repetition, i.e., the algorithms may create hundreds if not thousands of LPMs, and subsets of them are close in structure or behavior. Practically, no analyst would be able to comb through thousands of LPMs leading to using a sample of LPMs that are easily accessible. The current sentiment is that the top-scoring LPMs form the optimal sample to be presented. However, different applications should demand a different optimal sample. With this work, we show that if the goal of the mined LPMs is to understand a process, using the top-scoring LPMs as an optimal sample is a poor choice because of high repetition. We propose a framework for grouping LPMs and creating an optimal sample by taking one representative LPM for each group. We measure similarity between models via established process model similarity measures or by comparing the context in which an LPM appears. The context is formed using data attributes available in the underlying event logs. We demonstrate the usefulness of grouping on multiple event logs by comparing repetition and coverage between samples comprised of the top-scoring models and the representatives of discovered groups.

</details>


### [391] [Enhancing the Forecasting Capability of Multi-Model Blending Algorithms for Extreme Precipitation via Joint Use of Station and Gridded Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04862)
*Yu Wang,Yong Cao,Kan Dai,Yue Shen,Xiaoqing Zeng,Ruixia Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于U-Net的两阶段框架（概率分类+数值重建）来融合六个主要NWP模型的降水预报，并引入站-格点联合监督机制，显著提升了极端降水预报的准确性和空间校正能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值天气预报模型对极端降水存在系统性强度低估和空间偏移问题，传统多模型融合算法逐像素加权会导致降水区域扩大和极值平滑，亟需更有效的融合方法。

Method: 采用U-Net两阶段框架：第一阶段概率分类，第二阶段数值重建；创新性地引入站-格点联合监督机制，将全国2411个国家气象站观测数据融入损失函数，同时约束空间结构和峰值强度。

Result: 在2025年汛期独立样本评估中，暴雨（>=50mm）TS比最佳NWP提升38.4%；极端降水（>=100mm）TS提升至0.1以上，从无参考价值变为具有一定业务可用性；加入站点观测使暴雨TS进一步提升10.4%。

Conclusion: 多源联合监督机制能有效增强模型捕获极端降水事件的能力，通过数据驱动实现系统性的雨带空间校正，显著提升预报业务价值。

Abstract: Accurate extreme precipitation forecasting is critical for disaster mitigation but remains challenging for numerical weather prediction (NWP) models due to systemic intensity underestimation and spatial displacement. Traditional precipitation multi-model blending algorithms perform pixel-by-pixel blending on the forecast field based on weights, which may lead to the expansion of precipitation areas and the smoothing of extreme values. This study proposes an U-Net based two-stage framework: probability classification followed by value reconstruction, to blend forecasts from six major NWP models. A novel station-grid joint supervision mechanism is introduced by integrating observations from 2411 national meteorological stations in China into the loss function, simultaneously constraining spatial structures and peak intensities. Evaluations using independent samples from the 2025 flood season demonstrate that our model significantly outperforms both individual NWPs and current operational products. For rainstorms (>=50 mm), the Threat Score (TS) improved by 38.4% compared to the best NWP. Notably, for extreme events (>=100 mm) driven by extratropical cyclones and the subtropical high, the model successfully elevated the TS to above 0.1, transforming forecasts from having negligible reference value into those with certain operational utility. Furthermore, the model exhibits data-driven spatial correction capabilities, effectively realigning systematic rainbelt displacements with actual precipitation centers. The inclusion of station observations specifically enhanced the TS for rainstorms by 10.4% and effectively balanced the Bias. These results highlight the efficacy of multi-source joint supervision in enhancing the capture of extreme precipitation events.

</details>


### [392] [Active Learning on Adversarially Corrupted Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04869)
*Marco Bressan,Nicolò Cesa-Bianchi,Tommaso d`Orsi,Emmanuel Esposito,Silvio Lattanzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对恶意实体篡改网络图$G^*$（加入“腐败顶点”并添加边以隐藏它们）的场景，定义了一种对抗模型，并设计了一种主动学习算法，通过少量标签查询近似恢复被腐败的顶点。算法的查询复杂度与对抗者的能力和$G^*$的顶点扩展性（一种图连通性度量）呈多项式关系。这是首次证明顶点扩展性在对抗性结构攻击下主动学习的查询复杂度中起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中存在恶意实体篡改网络（如添加虚假节点或边）以隐藏其破坏行为的场景，亟需设计能够在这种对抗性结构攻击下高效识别被破坏顶点的算法。

Method: 将问题建模为对抗者向图$G^*$中添加“腐败顶点”并添加边（内部边及与$G^*$的边），以邻域大小衡量其能力；提出主动学习算法，核心是通过调整用于近似最小扩展的平方和（sum-of-squares）算法，在基数约束下找到具有小顶点扩展性的集合，从而近似恢复腐败顶点。

Result: 算法能够高效近似恢复腐败顶点，查询复杂度与对抗者能力（腐败顶点邻域大小）和$G^*$的顶点扩展性呈多项式关系。

Conclusion: 首次揭示了顶点扩展性在对抗性结构攻击下主动学习查询复杂度中的关键作用，为鲁棒图学习提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Motivated by real-world scenarios where malicious entities tamper with existing networks, we define a model where an adversary seeks to hide a set of \emph{corrupted vertices} inside a graph $G^*$. To this end, the adversary can add edges between the corrupted vertices, as well as edges between the corrupted vertices and $G^*$, and its power is then measured by the size of the \emph{neighborhood} of the corrupted vertices in $G^*$. Our goal is to design an active learning algorithm that efficiently finds the subset of corrupted vertices using a small number of label queries. We devise an efficient algorithm that approximately recovers the corrupted vertices with a query complexity that depends polynomially on both the power of the adversary and the \emph{vertex expansion} of $G^*$, a fundamental measure of graph connectivity. At the heart of this result is a polynomial-time algorithm, obtained by carefully adapting sum-of-squares algorithms for approximating minimum expansion, that finds a set with small vertex expansion subject to cardinality constraints. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first time that the vertex expansion is shown to play a key role in determining the query complexity of active learning algorithms robust to structural adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [393] [RL-Ballast: Ship Ballast Water Path Planning and Clog Prediction via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04906)
*Ming-Kuan Lin,Yi-Chung Lai,Ming-Hsin Chiang,Tsung-Wei Pan,Jung-Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RL-Ballast，一种基于图结构的深度强化学习框架，用于船舶压载水自适应路径规划与传感器稀疏条件下的堵塞故障诊断。在蒙特卡洛模拟中，该方法在单堵塞场景下决策步数从61.0降至41.5，Top-3命中率达100%，Top-1命中率66.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统压载水控制方法（规则式或手动）对液压异常（如阀门故障、管道堵塞）适应性不足，且依赖密集的压力或流量传感器进行故障诊断。为在传感器稀疏条件下实现自适应路径规划与故障定位，提出RL-Ballast框架。

Method: 1) 利用图论和深度优先搜索将阀门排列问题转化为54条可行流体传输路径；2) 采用帧堆叠的液位和动作结果近似部分可观测的压载环境，避免显式建模高维POMDP；3) 在确定性推理阶段引入回合级失败动作记忆与动态动作掩码，防止重复无效动作并支持即时重路由；4) 利用失败传输历史对可疑阀门/管道段进行排序评分。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟表明：RL-Ballast在所有意外单堵塞场景下均能完成目标，平均决策步数从61.0降至41.5；故障诊断方面，Top-3命中率100%，严格Top-1命中率66.7%，Top-1平局命中率83.3%。

Conclusion: RL-Ballast能够在有限传感条件下实现自适应重路由和面向维护的堵塞诊断，验证了图深度强化学习在智能船舶压载水系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Under the Shipping 4.0 paradigm, autonomous and reduced-crew vessels require intelligent internal systems to maintain operational safety and structural stability. Ballast-water control is essential for ship trim and integrity, but conventional rule-based or manual approaches have limited adaptability to hydraulic anomalies such as valve failures and pipe blockages, and often depend on dense pressure or flow sensors for diagnosis. To address these limitations, this paper proposes RL-Ballast, a graph-based deep reinforcement learning framework for adaptive ballast-water path planning and sensor-frugal blockage candidate scoring. The valve-permutation problem is transformed into 54 feasible fluid-transfer routes generated using graph theory and depth-first search. The partially observable ballast environment is approximated with frame-stacked tank levels and action outcomes, allowing the agent to infer hidden blockage effects without explicitly modeling a high-dimensional POMDP. During deterministic inference, episode-level failed-action memory and dynamic action masking prevent repeated ineffective actions and support immediate rerouting. Failed transfer histories are further accumulated to rank suspicious valves or pipe segments without dense instrumentation. Monte Carlo simulations show that RL-Ballast completes all unexpected single-blockage scenarios and reduces average decision steps from 61.0 to 41.5 compared with a Dijkstra rule-based baseline. For diagnostic support, the failure-history scoring scheme achieves a 100% Top-3 hit rate, a 66.7% strict Top-1 hit rate, and an 83.3% Top-1 tie-hit rate under serially indistinguishable blockage conditions. These results suggest that RL-Ballast enables adaptive rerouting and maintenance-oriented blockage diagnosis under limited sensing conditions.

</details>


### [394] [When Do Foundation Models Pay Off? A Break-Even Analysis of Pretrained Time Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04919)
*Nicholas Tan Jerome,Frank Simon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分析了时间序列基础模型（如Chronos、Moirai、Lag-Llama）相对于传统方法（Naive、ETS、ARIMA、XGBoost）的盈亏平衡点。在30个基准数据集上，基础模型在15个数据集上无条件优于传统方法；在其余数据集上，盈亏平衡点从24到8,361个样本不等。提出一个两阶段决策框架：若训练样本<700且季节性强，直接使用零样本基础模型；否则仅需5-10%的试点数据即可做出部署决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署时间序列基础模型需要GPU基础设施和工程成本，且无法保证优于XGBoost等传统方法。现有研究缺乏对"何时值得投入"的量化分析，本文旨在填补这一空白，提供首个系统性的盈亏平衡分析。

Method: 在30个基准数据集上，比较零样本和LoRA微调的基础模型（Chronos、Moirai、Lag-Llama）与经典基线（Naive、ETS、ARIMA、XGBoost）在6种训练集规模（从2%到100%）下的表现，进行盈亏平衡分析。

Result: 基础模型在15/30数据集上无条件优于传统方法；6个数据集上经典方法仅需2%训练数据即可超越零样本基础模型；9个数据集上盈亏平衡点在24-8,361样本之间。发现LoRA微调在短序列上可能降低性能。提出简单规则：n_train<700且有季节性的数据集，直接使用零样本基础模型。

Conclusion: 提出了一个两阶段决策框架，使从业者能够在投入全部基础设施之前，基于数据集长度和季节性强度做出基础模型与传统方法的选择。代码、基准测试和决策工具已开源。

Abstract: Deploying a time series foundation model requires GPU infrastructure, engineering overhead, and carries no guarantee of improvement over XGBoost. We provide the first systematic break-even analysis answering when this investment pays off. Across 30 benchmark datasets, we compare zero-shot and LoRA fine-tuned foundation models (Chronos, Moirai, Lag-Llama) against classical baselines (Naive, ETS, ARIMA, XGBoost) at six training set sizes from 2% to 100% of available data. Foundation models outperform classical methods at every evaluated training fraction on 15 of 30 datasets -- GPU deployment is unconditionally justified on these regardless of data volume. On 6 datasets, classical methods surpass zero-shot foundation models with as little as 2% of training data (21-2,768 samples); on the remaining 9, break-even ranges from 24 to 8,361 samples. One robust deployment rule requires no model training: if n_train < 700 and seasonality is non-negligible, use FM zero-shot and skip fine-tuning -- this resolves 10 of 30 deployment decisions immediately. Contrary to common practice, LoRA fine-tuning can actively degrade performance on short series. We operationalise these findings as a two-step decision framework -- compute dataset length and seasonality strength, run a brief 5-10% pilot only if needed -- enabling practitioners to make the FM-versus-classical decision before committing to full infrastructure. Four dataset features motivate mechanistic hypotheses for the remaining cases, though reliable automated prediction at this benchmark scale remains an open problem. Code, benchmark, and decision tools are available at https://github.com/nicolaisi/fm-breakeven.

</details>


### [395] [Input Pathways Shape Few-Shot, Not Zero-Shot, Binding in Tiny Transformers: A Fully-Enumerable Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04926)
*Yoshiyuki Ootani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过穷举有限因子世界，研究信息传递方式（符号token、干净Oracle编码、纠缠感知向量）如何影响Transformer的组合绑定能力。发现：端点不变性（零样本组合失败）、双因素解释（少样本绑定效率由输入路径参数共享和代码可读性决定）、双重分离（训练早期分布式编码短暂超随机水平，少样本效率追踪路径共享）、失败解剖（不同路径失败模式各异）。核心主张是双因素解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究信息到达Transformer的不同方式（符号token、Oracle编码、感知向量）如何影响模型将信息进行组合绑定的能力，即信息表示形式对组合泛化的影响。

Method: 在~6-10K参数的Transformer上穷举有限因子世界（全覆盖零采样方差），信息匹配精确贝叶斯上限1.0。通过端点不变性实验、双因素分析（参数共享与代码可读性）、维度匹配控制、可读性梯度扫描、训练早期追踪和失败解剖等方法系统研究。

Result: (1)所有信息路径在零样本组合任务上均未达到组合泛化（停留或低于随机水平）；(2)少样本绑定效率由输入路径参数共享和代码可读性共同解释，且干净Oracle并非最可读的路径；(3)分布式编码在训练早期短暂超过随机水平，而少样本效率追踪路径共享；(4)符号路径丢失答案，索引路径错误绑定，纠缠路径继承输入可读性。

Conclusion: 核心主张是双因素解释（参数共享和代码可读性解释少样本绑定），端点不变性和失败解剖提供诊断性约束。所有代码和日志开源以供复现。

Abstract: How does the way information reaches a transformer -- as symbolic tokens, a clean per-factor "oracle" code, or an entangled perceptual vector -- shape whether it binds that information compositionally? We study ~6-10K-parameter transformers on finite factored worlds enumerated exhaustively, so every measurement covers the whole input space (zero sampling variance) and the informative routes are information-matched (exact Bayes ceiling 1.0). We report four findings. (1) Endpoint invariance: on held-out binding queries no informative route reaches converged zero-shot composition -- each ends at or below chance despite a ceiling of 1.0, so within a bounded sweep the failure reflects inductive bias under a lookup-sufficient objective, not missing information. (2) A two-factor account of few-shot binding: sample efficiency is best explained by input-pathway parameter sharing and code readability; a dimension-matched control and a graded readability sweep isolate readability from input dimension, and the clean oracle is not the most sample-efficient readable route. (3) A double dissociation: early in training, distributed -- but not index-like -- codes pass through a transient above-chance phase (tracking code format), while few-shot efficiency tracks pathway sharing. (4) Failure anatomy: symbolic routes lose the answer at the readout; index routes mis-bind (the answer stays decodable, yet an input intervention shows the output tracks the wrong slot); entangled routes inherit their input's readability. The central claim is the two-factor account; the endpoint and anatomy results are diagnostic constraints. All code, manifests, and per-seed logs are released for exact reproduction.

</details>


### [396] [Lightweight ML-Based Automatic Sleep Staging Framework with Constrained CNN and Mamba for Small-Sample EEG Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04934)
*Zihao Wei,Yulin Gong,Yudan Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GamSleepNet是一个轻量级低延迟的单通道EEG自动睡眠分期框架，仅用3.086万参数在Sleepedf数据集上达到87.86%准确率，所有指标达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动睡眠分期方法存在三大挑战：参数量大导致小数据集过拟合、N1和REM等困难分期准确率低、最优训练集规模不明确且难以部署。

Method: 提出GamSleepNet框架，包含三个核心创新：1) FEB模块，融合改进的Gabor核与可学习滤波器进行特征提取；2) 基于Mamba架构构建时序分类网络；3) 引入新的对比损失和两阶段训练策略，并实验验证了单通道EEG睡眠分期模型的最优数据集规模。

Result: 在Sleepedf数据集上，模型仅用3.086万参数即达到87.86%的整体准确率，所有指标均达到SOTA水平，显著提升了困难睡眠分期的识别准确率。

Conclusion: GamSleepNet有效解决了当前自动睡眠分期方法的参数过大、困难分期识别率低和部署困难等问题，为便携式EEG设备的自动睡眠分期提供了高效可行的轻量化解决方案。

Abstract: Automatic sleep staging is a key technology for precise diagnosis and treatment of sleep disorders as well as long-term home sleep monitoring. Portable electroencephalogram (EEG) devices have become the focus of research due to their convenience in data collection. However, current methods still face three major challenges: large parameter sizes that easily lead to overfitting on small datasets, low accuracy in classifying difficult stages such as N1 and REM, unclear optimal training dataset size, and difficulty in deployment. This paper proposes GamSleepNet, a lightweight and low-latency automatic sleep staging framework for single-channel EEG. The framework features the FEB module, which combines improved Gabor kernels with learnable filters for feature extraction, uses the Mamba architecture to build a temporal classification network, introduces a novel contrastive loss and a two-stage training strategy, and experimentally validates the optimal dataset size for single-channel EEG sleep staging models. On the Sleepedf dataset, this model achieves an overall accuracy of 87.86 percent with only 30.86 thousand parameters, with all metrics reaching SOTA levels and significantly improving the identification accuracy of challenging sleep stages.

</details>


### [397] [Sensitivity Sampling with Predictions for k-Means Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04949)
*Cristian Boldrin,Fabio Vandin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用预测来近似敏感度采样中的重要性估计，从而加速大规模数据集上的k-means聚类。理论证明即使较粗糙的近似也足以保证coreset质量，并设计了一种基于历史聚类中心作为预测的自然方法，实验表明该方法在聚类代价与运行时间之间取得了更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于敏感度采样的coreset方法虽然能提供高质量近似解，但计算每个数据点敏感度的时间开销过大，难以应用于大规模数据集。

Method: 1) 理论证明当前敏感度采样的理论结果对更粗糙的敏感度近似仍然成立；2) 提出利用预测（如来自同一问题历史数据集的聚类中心）来近似敏感度值；3) 对来自同一未知分布的数据序列，证明用前一个数据集的最优中心作为当前数据集的预测具有理论保证。

Result: 实验表明，在处理数据序列时，所提方法在聚类代价与运行时间的权衡上显著优于均匀采样和现有的敏感度采样方法。

Conclusion: 利用预测可以大幅降低敏感度采样的计算开销，同时保证coreset的质量，为大规模k-means聚类提供了更实用的方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of k-means clustering on large datasets. The state-of-the-art for the problem is given by coresets-based approaches, which build small weighted summaries of the input and derive approximate solutions with rigorous quality guarantees from them. One of the most popular and advanced approaches to derive coresets for k-means is sensitivity sampling. However, sensitivity sampling requires to compute the importance of each input point with respect to the whole dataset over all possible choices of centers. Since the exact computation of such quantities is unfeasible, current approaches work by approximating the sensitivity values. Nevertheless, the runtime of such approaches is still impractical for large datasets.
  In this work, we propose to reduce the runtime of sensitivity-based approaches for k-means by leveraging predictions to approximate the importance of input points. We first formally prove that current theoretical results on coresets construction via sensitivity sampling hold for coarser approximations of sensitivities compared to the one required by existing approaches. This implies that even fairly noisy predictors can be leveraged for sensitivity-sampling approaches. We then propose a natural predictor, which applies to the common scenario where clustering is performed (over time) on a sequence of datasets from the same problem. We prove that when the datasets in the sequence come from the same (unknown) distribution, centers resulting in a low error on one dataset can be used as predictions for sensitivity sampling in subsequent datasets, with guarantees on their quality. We perform an extensive experimental evaluation showing that our approach significantly improves, in terms of clustering cost vs runtime, over uniform sampling and state-of-the-art sensitivity sampling approaches when applied to sequences of datasets.

</details>


### [398] [Geometry-Aware Bayesian Quantification via Compositional Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04977)
*Alejandro Moreo,Pablo González,Juan José del Coz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Aitchison几何和log-ratio表示的几何感知KDE模型，用于多类量化（标签分布估计），解决了传统欧几里得高斯核忽略概率单形体几何结构的问题，并在42个数据集上取得了竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于KDE的量化方法使用欧几里得高斯核，忽略了后验向量位于概率单形体上的几何结构，导致概率质量错误地分配到单形体边界之外，影响估计精度。

Method: 提出几何感知KDE模型：采用log-ratio表示和Aitchison几何替代欧几里得几何；引入收缩正则化提高近边界区域的鲁棒性；结合最大似然解释，推导了点估计和贝叶斯推断两种类别先验估计方法。

Result: 在42个数据集（涵盖表格、文本和图像领域）上的实验表明，该方法与最先进的量化器竞争，通常优于标准KDE基线，同时在贝叶斯量化方法中也取得强结果。

Conclusion: 考虑单形体几何结构的KDE方法能有效提升多类量化性能，尤其在后验向量靠近边界时，几何感知和收缩正则化是关键改进点。

Abstract: Accurately estimating the unknown target label distribution is the critical first step for adapting to label shift. This task, widely known as quantification or class prevalence estimation, has recently seen significant advances through continuous KDE-based methods which model the density of multiclass classifier posteriors. Posterior vectors might be regarded as compositional data, since they lie on the probability simplex. However, existing KDE-based quantifiers typically rely on Euclidean Gaussian kernels, which ignore simplex geometry and incorrectly assign probability mass outside its boundaries. We introduce a geometry-aware KDE model for multiclass quantification based on log-ratio representations and Aitchison geometry, together with a shrinkage regularization that improves robustness near the simplex boundary. Combined with a maximum-likelihood interpretation of KDE-based quantification, we derive both point-estimation and Bayesian inference procedures for class prevalences. Experiments on 42 datasets across tabular, text, and image domains show that the proposed method is competitive with state-of-the-art quantifiers, often improving over standard KDE-based baselines, while also yielding strong results among Bayesian quantification methods.

</details>


### [399] [Qantara: Bridge-Flow Training for Multi-Paradigm JEPA Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04978)
*Ruslan Rakhimov,George Bredis,Yuriy Maksyuta,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Qantara是一种联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)，通过联合训练目标（状态轴上的布朗桥插值与动作轴上的噪声到数据流匹配），使同一个检查点支持三种推理范式（潜在规划、行为克隆动作采样、逆动力学），无需重新训练。在LeWM控制套件上达到91.2 SR，在OGBench-Cube上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA世界模型在训练时只能选择单一推理范式（轨迹优化或行为克隆），无法在推理时根据部署约束灵活切换。作者希望实现一个检查点支持多种推理范式。

Method: 提出Qantara，端到端JEPA架构。联合训练目标包含两部分：状态轴上连续干净潜变量间的布朗桥插值，以及动作轴上的噪声到数据流匹配。训练集中分布在(动作-时间, 状态-时间)噪声平方的边缘区域。

Result: Push-T规划达到90.1 SR（均匀内部采样降至53.3 SR）；LeWM控制套件平均91.2 SR；OGBench-Cube上+7.7 SR超越DINO-WM，+19.7超越LeWM；行为克隆和视频逆路径在Push-T上达82-83 SR，Cube上达71-73 SR。

Conclusion: Qantara将JEPA世界模型从单范式规划器转变为多范式控制器，同一个权重可服务于规划、行为克隆和逆动力学三种推理范式。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) underpin a growing family of latent world models for control from raw pixels, but every existing JEPA world model commits at training time to a single inference paradigm: either trajectory optimisation in a learned dynamics model, or direct behaviour cloning. A single checkpoint that serves both would defer this choice to inference, when deployment constraints (rollout cost, observation accessibility) determine which path wins. We present Qantara, an end-to-end JEPA whose joint training objective pairs a Brownian-bridge interpolant between consecutive clean latents on the state axis with noise-to-data flow matching on the action axis. The same checkpoint serves three inference paradigms without retraining: latent planning, behaviour-cloning action sampling, and inverse dynamics, which we query through a video-inverse composition that first predicts the next latent without action conditioning, then extracts the action. Training concentrates mass on the edges of the (action-time, state-time) noise square, where inference queries the predictor: replacing it with uniform interior sampling drops Push-T planning from 90.1 to 53.3 SR at matched compute. On the LeWM control suite, Qantara reaches a 91.2 SR three-train-seed average and sets new SOTA on OGBench-Cube (+7.7 SR over DINO-WM, +19.7 over LeWM). From the same weights, the behaviour-cloning and video-inverse paths reach 82-83 SR on Push-T and 71-73 SR on Cube. These results move JEPA world models from single-paradigm planners to multi-paradigm controllers.

</details>


### [400] [Data-Driven Soft Labeling Scales DNA Read Classification to Whole-Body Cell-Type Deconvolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04987)
*Dmytro Rizdvanetskyi,Nathan Ross,Pavlo Lutsik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Syto框架，通过数据驱动的软标签解决DNA甲基化模式与细胞类型间的多对多映射问题，在39种人类细胞类型上实现2.56倍的MSE降低，并适用于大规模细胞类型面板的去卷积任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于DNA甲基化的细胞类型去卷积方法多使用聚合甲基化估计，丢弃了单条DNA读段携带的模式级信息。已有的读级方法局限于少数类别场景，在大规模场景下非判别性读段占主导，且硬标签与多对多映射冲突导致分类器无法收敛。

Method: 提出数据驱动的软标签机制，估计每条读段的条件细胞类型分布；将其集成到Syto模块化框架中，实现基于读级分类的去卷积。

Result: 在39种人类细胞类型的全身图谱上，Syto将均方误差（MSE）降低了2.56倍；在涵盖16种组织的外部分布数据集上性能迁移良好。

Conclusion: Syto为建模日益庞大的细胞类型面板奠定了基础，改进了生物学和医疗应用；所提出的软标签方案可推广到任何具有多对多信号到标签映射的场景。

Abstract: Cell-type deconvolution, the task of estimating the proportions of constituent cell types in a heterogeneous biological sample, is a core problem in computational biology. Methods that rely on epigenetic marks such as DNA methylation typically operate on aggregated methylation estimates, discarding the pattern-level information carried by individual DNA reads. Existing read-level approaches that exploit this information are scarce, and all remain restricted to few-class settings; scaling them further is an open problem because, at scale, non-discriminative reads dominate and hard labels conflict with the many-to-many mapping between methylation patterns and cell types, preventing classifier convergence. To overcome this, we propose data-driven soft labels that estimate the conditional cell-type distribution for each read, and integrate this scheme into Syto, a new modular framework for read-level classification-based deconvolution. On a whole-body atlas of 39 human cell types, Syto reduces MSE by 2.56$\times$ over SoTA, with gains transferring to an out-of-distribution dataset spanning 16 tissues. Syto lays the foundation for modeling increasingly large cell-type panels, with improved applications in biology and healthcare. The proposed soft-labeling scheme is further translatable to any setting with a many-to-many signal-to-label mapping.

</details>


### [401] [Non-Convex Sparse Reinforcement Learning via Non-Monotone Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04990)
*Kyohei Suzuki,onstantinos Slavakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将非凸PMC惩罚项引入LSTD策略评估，并利用前向-反射-后向分裂（FRBS）方法求解非单调包含问题，在强化学习特征选择中取得了显著优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统正则化方法在强化学习特征选择中存在估计偏差问题，且由于PMC惩罚的弱凸性导致固定点问题不再单调，需要新的理论工具来保证求解算法的收敛性。

Method: 将非凸PMC惩罚项增强经典LSTD策略评估，将问题建模为非单调包含（单调Lipschitz算子+次单调算子之和）；采用前向-反射-后向分裂（FRBS）方法求解，并在弱Minty变分不等式假设下建立收敛性理论。

Result: 在基准数据集上，FRBS迭代应用于非凸正则化LSTD问题，显著优于最先进的特征选择方法，尤其在存在大量噪声特征时优势更为突出。

Conclusion: 本文在强化学习特征选择与非单调包含理论两方面均做出了贡献：为RL提供了有效的非凸正则化特征选择方法，为非单调包含问题建立了新的FRBS收敛条件。

Abstract: This work delivers two key contributions: one to efficient feature selection in reinforcement learning
  (RL), the other to the theory of non-monotone inclusions. On the RL side, the estimation bias inherent
  in conventional regularization schemes is addressed by augmenting classical least-squares
  temporal-difference (LSTD) policy evaluation with the sparsity-inducing, non-convex projected minimax
  concave (PMC) penalty. Because the PMC penalty is weakly convex, the resulting fixed-point problem is
  no longer monotone; instead, it falls under a broader class of non-monotone inclusions involving the
  sum of a monotone Lipschitz operator and a hypomonotone operator. On the theory side, novel convergence
  conditions are developed for the forward-reflected-backward splitting (FRBS) method applied to this
  broader class of non-monotone inclusion problems. Under mild conditions, Lyapunov stability and the
  existence of a limit point of the sequence of FRBS iterates are established; alternatively, under the
  weak Minty variational inequality assumption, exact convergence is guaranteed. Numerical tests on
  benchmark datasets show that the proposed FRBS iterates, applied to the non-convexly regularized LSTD
  problem, substantially outperform state-of-the-art feature-selection methods, especially when many
  noisy features are present.

</details>


### [402] [The Map Behind the Flow: Finite-Step Gradient Descent as a Dynamical System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04993)
*Thomas Hofmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究固定步长梯度下降作为离散动力系统的动力学行为，通过一系列可精确求解的模型，揭示了有限步长导致的边缘稳定性、震荡、平衡化等动力学现象，并证明学习率不仅是数值稳定性参数，更是训练动力学的结构性参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的许多现象（如边缘稳定性、震荡、 catapult 阶段、平衡化等）本质上是训练映射本身的动力学效应，小步长梯度流极限难以捕捉这些现象。现有研究缺乏对固定步长梯度下降作为离散动力系统的深入理解。

Method: 构建从浅到深、逐步增加复杂性的可精确求解模型层次（深度线性链→标量约化→因子化模型→宽线性网络→数据耦合、非线性激活、随机目标），分析其离散动力系统行为。

Result: 深度线性链在自然大深度缩放下收敛到通用的Ricker型映射，边缘稳定性是训练映射的第一个分岔点。有限步长打破了梯度流的守恒律，残差振荡推动参数向更平坦、更平衡的表征移动。宽线性网络产生谱边缘阶梯，最优学习率可能位于第一边缘之后。

Conclusion: 学习率不仅是数值稳定性参数，更是训练动力学的结构性参数，它决定了吸引子并塑造了梯度下降选择的表征。

Abstract: Many phenomena of deep learning are dynamical: they concern not only which minima exist, but how gradient descent reaches, avoids, or selects among them. Edge-of-stability behavior, sharpness oscillations, catapult phases, balancing, and movement toward flatter representations are effects of the training map itself, and are poorly captured by the small-step gradient-flow limit.
  This paper studies fixed-step gradient descent as a discrete dynamical system in a hierarchy of exactly solvable models retaining basic structures of deep learning: depth, factorization, width, data coupling, activation, and stochasticity. The starting point is the balanced scalar reduction of a deep linear chain, giving a quartic loss and a cubic gradient map whose post-edge behavior is explicit. Under the natural large-depth scaling, this dynamics converges to a universal Ricker-type map. The edge of stability is therefore not a breakdown of optimization, but the first bifurcation of the training map.
  Embedding the scalar dynamics back into factored models turns these regimes into learning phenomena. Finite steps break conservation laws of gradient flow and contract factorization imbalance; residual oscillations move parameters toward flatter, more balanced representations. Wider linear networks produce a ladder of spectral edges, so the optimal learning rate can lie beyond the first edge. Data coupling, nonlinear activations, and stochastic targets preserve the same organizing principle: finite-step oscillations drive alignment, balancing, and representation selection. Thus the learning rate is not merely a numerical stability parameter. It is a structural parameter of the training dynamics, determining its attractors and shaping the representations gradient descent selects.

</details>


### [403] [ImputeECG: Deep Learning Reconstruction of Complete 12-Lead Electrocardiograms from Incomplete Recordings for Cardiac Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05009)
*Xiaocheng Fang,Haoyu Wang,Jieyi Cai,Qinghao Zhao,Jun Li,Shanwei Zhang,Guangkun Nie,Yujie Xiao,Shun Huang,Jiarui Jin,Hongmin Liu,Guodong Wang,Shuohua Chen,Liming Lin,Shouling Wu,Hongyan Li,Shenda Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ImputeECG，一种基于掩码条件的一维Transformer自编码器，用于补全不完整的12导联10秒心电图信号，在多个数据集上显著降低重建误差，并提升下游诊断任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多临床心电图记录（尤其是从图像数字化而来的）因显示格式短、波形数字化不完整、导联缺失或信号损坏而不完整，阻碍了AI辅助心血管评估的发展。

Method: 开发了ImputeECG，一个掩码条件的一维Transformer自编码器，在保留所有可观测样本的同时补全12导联10秒心电图。

Result: 在PTB-XL上，MAE降低41.7-51.0%，MSE降低54.0-63.7%；在CPSC2018上MAE降低49.7-51.9%；下游分类任务AUROC恢复到92.28%；真实临床队列中性别预测AUROC从82.6%提升至85.8%，年龄预测MAE从10.72降至9.87年。

Conclusion: 心电图补全是将不完整ECG记录转化为AI可用数字信号的有效策略，可扩展心电图档案的可用范围。

Abstract: Complete digital 12-lead electrocardiograms (ECGs) are essential for AI-enabled cardiovascular assessment, yet many clinical ECG records, particularly those digitized from ECG images, remain incomplete because of short display formats, incomplete waveform digitization, lead loss, or signal corruption. We developed ImputeECG, a mask-conditioned one-dimensional Transformer autoencoder that completes 12-lead, 10-s ECGs while retaining all observed samples. The model was trained on PTB-XL and evaluated on PTB-XL and CPSC2018 under simulated incomplete settings, with additional real-world validation in a 43,633-record Kailuan clinical cohort after ECG image digitization. Metrics were computed over originally missing regions, with analyses of morphology and downstream diagnostic utility. On PTB-XL, ImputeECG reduced missing-region MAE by 41.7-51.0% and MSE by 54.0-63.7% versus the strongest baseline, with lower errors in R-peak timing, RR interval, QRS duration, QT interval, and P-wave, QRS-complex, and T-wave reconstruction. On CPSC2018, ImputeECG reduced MAE by 49.7-51.9%, supporting external generalization. In downstream multi-label classification, ImputeECG restored performance to 92.28% AUROC and 33.88% AUPRC in the most incomplete PTB-XL setting, approaching complete-ECG performance. On CPSC2018, completed ECGs achieved 94.75-95.89% AUROC and 78.83-81.86% AUPRC across settings. In Kailuan, ECG completion improved zero-shot sex prediction AUROC from 82.6% to 85.8% and reduced age prediction MAE from 10.72 to 9.87 years after image-based ECG digitization. These findings support ECG completion as a practical strategy for converting incomplete ECG records into AI-ready 12-lead, 10-s digital signals and extending the usable scope of ECG archives for digital cardiac assessment.

</details>


### [404] [Hyperparameter Transfer in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05017)
*Gage DeZoort,Boris Hanin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了图神经网络（GNNs）的超参数迁移问题，提出了适用于SGD、Adam和AdamW优化器的迁移参数化方案，使超参数设置（如学习率、权重衰减）在不同模型规模间保持最优，并通过理论分析和实验验证了其在宽度和深度增加时性能的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超参数迁移在密集神经网络中已有深入研究，但在图神经网络（GNNs）中相对未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，使大规模GNNs可以通过优化其小规模对应模型来间接调优。

Method: 通过理论缩放分析和控制实验，针对SGD、Adam和AdamW三种优化器分别设计迁移参数化方案。对SGD提出图相关第一层校正因子，对Adam研究消息传递归一化对迁移行为的影响，对AdamW实现权重衰减和学习率的联合迁移。

Result: 所提参数化方案实现了稳定的特征更新、学习率迁移，以及随宽度和深度增加的性能提升。SGD中图相关校正因子可加速稀疏词袋输入图上的早期训练；Adam中消息传递归一化对迁移行为至关重要；AdamW实现了权重衰减与学习率的联合迁移。

Conclusion: 本文为GNNs在不同学习任务和训练场景下的缩放提供了实用方案，验证了超参数迁移在GNNs中的有效性和重要性。

Abstract: The performance of deep learning models crucially depends on the settings of hyperparameters like learning rate, initialization scale, and weight decay. Hyperparameter transfer aims to make near-optimal hyperparameter settings consistent across model scale, so that large models can be optimized by proxy tuning their smaller, cheaper-to-optimize counterparts. While transfer principles are well-studied in the context of dense neural networks in language and vision tasks, they remain comparatively under-explored for graph neural networks (GNNs). We develop and validate a transfer parameterization for GNNs trained with SGD, Adam, and AdamW. Through theoretical scaling analyses and controlled experiments, we show that the proposed parameterization yields stable feature updates, learning rate transfer, and improved performance as width and depth increase. For SGD, we identify graph-dependent first-layer correction factors and show that their use can accelerate early training in graphs with sparse bag-of-words inputs. For Adam, we explore how different message passing normalizations affect early- and late-training transfer behavior, illustrating the importance of message passing normalization and advocating for an associated hyperparameter. For AdamW, we adapt a parameterization that allows for the joint transfer of weight decay and learning rate. Together, these results provide a practical recipe for scaling GNNs across a variety of learning tasks and training scenarios.

</details>


### [405] [Beyond Modality Fusion: Deep Ensembles for Multimodal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05019)
*Ilya Burenko,Dmitry Vetrov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，在模态不平衡的情况下，使用单模态网络的深度集成（Deep Ensembles）无需显式融合即可在多模态分类中超越传统的晚期融合（Late-Fusion）和中间融合（Intermediate-Fusion）方法。研究提出了一个启发式方法来选择每个模态的模型数量，并提出了合成多模态框架来系统探索模型优化的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统晚期融合方法在模态不平衡时性能不佳，尽管已有多种正则化技术尝试平衡学习过程，但本文旨在证明无需显式模态融合，仅靠单模态网络的集成就能有效分类多模态数据。

Method: 系统比较深度集成与晚期融合网络（参数相同）；提出启发式方法选择每模态模型数量；提出合成多模态框架以控制模态数量和预测强度；拟合缩放定律估计集成方法的渐近性能。

Result: 深度集成在参数相同的情况下始终优于最先进的晚期融合和中间融合方法；极端不平衡时，仅使用强模态的模型集成更优；规模扩大后，加入弱模态模型有益；合成和真实数据集上结果一致。
